--- Commit Message ---
LU-19921 llite: add OBD_NOTIFY_GSSIAM upcall

Add OBD_NOTIFY_GSSIAM notification event handler in llite,
so ptlrpc can fetch IAM mount directory and option from llite.

Replace sbi with sb in obd_upcall.onu_owner, so readonly
option can be detected from sb during gssiam upcall.

Cache the login UID of the mount process into sbi (as ll_loginuid).
This is specifically needed for GSSIAM authentication (passed down
via upcalls), as it cannot be dynamically retrieved inside
ll_upcall_notify_gssiam(). When the security context is
re-established asynchronously during server failover or reconnect
recovery, the upcall notification runs in the context of
background ptlrpcd kernel threads where the active login UID
cannot be resolved (resulting in -1). Caching it during mount
ensures the original mount-time login UID is preserved for
recovery.

Signed-off-by: Di Wang <ddiwang@google.com>

(3 comments)

Experimental feature testing in progress, please pay attention to the comments

Mostly infrastructure for the GSS-IAM feature (LU-19921). The reused ll_upcall_notify() path for the existing OSC OBD_NOTIFY_ACTIVE handling looks equivalent to the old cl_ocd_update() registration.

One thing to double-check: on the success path ll_upcall_notify_gssiam() stores a freshly allocated lustre_gssiam_mount_info into imp_sec->ps_gssiam, but nothing in the tree frees ps_gssiam, and nothing reads the lgmi_* fields yet. OBD_NOTIFY_GSSIAM is also never fired by any in-tree caller, so this handler and the cached state are not exercised by the current series. Is the producer/consumer (and the free) landing in a follow-on patch on the same ticket?
